The reset vector entry is at offset 0x80.\n- **mstatus (0x300):** `timer_enable()` sets bit 3 (MIE=1) via `csrs mstatus, 0x8` to enable global interrupts.\n- **mie (0x304):** `timer_enable()` sets bit 7 (MTIE=1) via `csrs mie, 0x80` to enable the timer interrupt.\n- **mepc (0x341):** Hardware saves PC on interrupt entry, restored by MRET.\n- **mcause (0x342):** Set by hardware on interrupt/exception entry.\n- **mcountinhibit (0x320):** `pcount_enable()` writes 0 or 0xFFFFFFFF to CSR 0x320.\n\n**MMIO required:**\n- Write to 0x20000 (SIM_CTRL_OUT) for every character in puts/puthex/putchar.\n- Read from 0x30000/0x30004 (TIMER_MTIME/MTIMEH) to read current timer value.\n- Write to 0x30008/0x3000C (TIMER_MTIMECMP/MTIMECMPH) to set next compare value.\n\n**WFI behavior:** The core must suspend and wake on `irq_timer_i` assertion when `mstatus.MIE=1` and `mie[7]=1`.\n\n**Interrupt vector layout (from crt0.S):**\n\n```\noffset 0x00 - 0x1B: default_exc_handler entries (7 x jal x0)\noffset 0x1C:        timer_handler (jal x0, simple_timer_handler)  <-- ID 7, base+4*7\noffset 0x20 - 0x7F: default_exc_handler entries (23 more)\noffset 0x80:        reset vector (jal x0, reset_handler)\n```\n\nmtvec must have MODE=01 (vectored). On timer interrupt (ID=7), core jumps to mtvec.BASE + 4*7 = BASE + 0x1C.\n\n---\n\n#### 2. dit_test (`/app/examples/sw/simple_system/dit_test/dit_test.c`)\n\n**What it does:** Verifies data-independent timing by timing branch-heavy and divide-heavy functions with DIT on/off. Also tests with ICache on.\n\n**CSRs required:**\n\n- **cpuctrlsts (0x7C0):** All accesses use `csrsi`/`csrci` with immediate masks:\n  - `csrsi 0x7c0, 0x2` -- sets bit 1 (data_ind_timing = DIT on)\n  - `csrci 0x7c0, 0x2` -- clears bit 1 (DIT off)\n  - `csrsi 0x7c0, 0x1` -- sets bit 0 (icache_enable on)\n  - `csrci 0x7c0, 0x1` -- clears bit 0 (icache_enable off)\n- **mcountinhibit (0x320):** `pcount_enable(1)` writes 0 to enable counters.\n- **mcycle (0xB00) / mcycleh (0xB80):** The timing functions (count_ones_zeros, div_ones_zeros) read cycle counts to measure execution time.\n\n**cpuctrlsts bit fields used:**\n\n| Bit | Name | Action |\n|---|---|---|\n| 0 | icache_enable | Enable/disable instruction cache |\n| 1 | data_ind_timing | Enable/disable DIT (the \"DIT bit\") |\n\n**Behavioral requirement:** When DIT=1, branch and divide instruction latency must become data-independent (constant). When DIT=0, at least 25% of iterations must show different timing.\n\n---\n\n#### 3. dummy_instr_test (`/app/examples/sw/simple_system/dummy_instr_test/dummy_instr_test.c`)\n\n**What it does:** Times a `busy_work` function. With dummy instructions off, all runs must take equal time. With dummy instructions on, all runs must take different time.\n\n**CSRs required:**\n\n- **cpuctrlsts (0x7C0):**\n  - `csrsi 0x7c0, 0x4` -- sets bit 2 (dummy_instr_en = 1, on)\n  - `csrci 0x7c0, 0x4` -- clears bit 2 (dummy_instr_en = 0, off)\n- **mcycle (0xB00):** The `busy_work` function uses cycle count reads for timing (returns cycle delta).\n- **mcountinhibit (0x320):** `pcount_enable(1)` enables counters.\n\n**cpuctrlsts bit fields used:**\n\n| Bit | Name | Action |\n|---|---|---|\n| 2 | dummy_instr_en | Enable/disable random dummy instruction insertion |\n\n**Note:** The dummy_instr_mask (bits 5:3) controls frequency; the test only toggles bit 2. The hardware must actually insert multiply/divide dummy instructions pseudo-randomly when enabled, causing cycle-count variation.\n\n---\n\n#### 4. pmp_smoke_test (`/app/examples/sw/simple_system/pmp_smoke_test/pmp_smoke_test.c`)\n\n**What it does:** Configures PMP region 0 as locked read-only (NA4 mode) over a test variable, then attempts to write to it (expecting a store access fault).\n\n**CSRs required:**\n\n- **pmpaddr0 (0x3B0):** Written with `((unsigned int)&test_int) >> 2` (physical address right-shifted by 2, per the `pmpaddrx` format storing address[33:2]).\n- **pmpcfg0 (0x3A0):** Written with `0x91`:\n  ```\n  0x91 = 0b_1001_0001\n    bit 7 = 1  --> Lock bit set (region locked, applies to M-mode too)\n    bit 6:5 = 00 --> Reserved\n    bit 4:3 = 10 --> Mode = NA4 (Naturally Aligned 4-byte region)\n    bit 2 = 0  --> No execute permission\n    bit 1 = 0  --> No write permission\n    bit 0 = 1  --> Read permission only\n  ```\n- **mcause (0x342):** The exception handler (via crt0.S default_exc_handler) reads this; store access fault = exception code 7.\n- **mepc (0x341):** Read by exception handler for diagnostic output.\n- **mtval (0x343):** Read by exception handler (holds faulting address on store fault).\n\n**PMP module requirement:** PMPEnable parameter must be non-zero. The PMP unit must intercept the store to `test_int` after pmpcfg0 is written with the locked read-only config, and generate a store access fault (exception code 7).\n\n---\n\n### Summary: Minimum CSR Set to Implement\n\nFor all 4 tests to pass, the RTL must implement these CSRs at minimum:\n\n| Address | Name | Priority |\n|---|---|---|\n| 0x300 | mstatus (MIE, MPIE, MPP bits) | Critical |\n| 0x304 | mie (MTIE bit 7) | Critical |\n| 0x305 | mtvec (vectored mode, MODE=01) | Critical |\n| 0x320 | mcountinhibit | Needed |\n| 0x341 | mepc | Critical |\n| 0x342 | mcause | Critical |\n| 0x343 | mtval | Needed |\n| 0x344 | mip | Needed |\n| 0x3A0 | pmpcfg0 | pmp_smoke_test |\n| 0x3B0 | pmpaddr0 | pmp_smoke_test |\n| 0x7C0 | cpuctrlsts (bits 0, 1, 2) | dit_test, dummy_instr_test |\n| 0xB00 | mcycle | dit_test, dummy_instr_test |\n| 0xB80 | mcycleh | dit_test, dummy_instr_test |\n| 0xB02 | minstret | pcount_reset |\n| 0xB82 | minstreth | pcount_reset |\n| 0xB03-0xB1F | mhpmcounter3-31 | pcount_reset |\n| 0xB83-0xB9F | mhpmcounter3h-31h | pcount_reset |\n\nKey source files for reference:\n- `/app/doc/03_reference/cs_registers.rst`\n- `/app/doc/03_reference/exception_interrupts.rst`\n- Ibex RISC-V Core Implementation Plan\n\n## Context\n\nAll RTL implementation files in `/app/rtl/` and the SoC wrapper have been stripped to empty.\nThe task is to implement the Ibex RISC-V CPU core so that 4 software simulation tests pass:\n1. `hello_test` — basic I/O, timer interrupts, WFI\n2. `dit_test` — data-independent timing via CPUCTRLSTS CSR (0x7C0)\n3. `dummy_instr_test` — random dummy instruction insertion via CPUCTRLSTS\n4. `pmp_smoke_test` — PMP region enforcement (passes even without PMP since PMPEnable=0 default)\n\n## Default Build Configuration (from .vc file)\n\n```\nRV32E=0, ICache=0, ICacheScramble=0, ICacheECC=0\nBranchTargetALU=0, WritebackStage=0, SecureIbex=0\nBranchPredictor=0, DbgTriggerEn=0\nPMPEnable=0, PMPGranularity=0, PMPNumRegions=4\nMHPMCounterNum=0, MHPMCounterWidth=40\nRVFI=1 (defined), RV32M=RV32MFast, RV32B=RV32BNone\nRV32ZC=RV32ZcaZcbZcmp, RegFile=RegFileFF\n```\n\n## Critical Findings\n\n- **pmp_smoke_test**: With PMPEnable=0 (default), PMP CSR writes are ignored, store succeeds normally, main returns 0 → PASSES\n- **dit_test with ICache**: requires DIT timing to be constant even without real ICache (works if instruction fetches are always 1-cycle in simulation)\n- **dummy_instr_test**: requires LFSR-based random dummy instruction insertion causing cycle-count variation\n- **hello_test**: requires timer interrupt at ID 7 (vectored mtvec: BASE+4*7), WFI, MMIO I/O\n\n## Implementation Order (bottom-up dependency)\n\n### Phase 1: Packages and Primitives\n1. `rtl/ibex_pkg.sv` — All enums, types, params. **Implement first.**\n2. `rtl/ibex_tracer_pkg.sv` — Tracer enum constants\n\n### Phase 2: Leaf Modules (no sub-module deps)\n3. `rtl/ibex_alu.sv` — ADD/SUB/AND/OR/XOR/shifts/compare, 32-bit\n4. `rtl/ibex_compressed_decoder.sv` — RVC→RVI expansion\n5. `rtl/ibex_decoder.sv` — 32-bit instruction decode → control signals\n6. `rtl/ibex_register_file_ff.sv` — 32×32 register file (FF-based)\n7. `rtl/ibex_register_file_fpga.sv` — FPGA variant (same interface, can be alias)\n8. `rtl/ibex_register_file_latch.sv` — Latch variant (same interface)\n9. `rtl/ibex_branch_predict.sv` — Stub (BranchPredictor=0 default)\n10. `rtl/ibex_csr.sv` — Single CSR primitive (WARL register with mask)\n11. `rtl/ibex_counter.sv` — 64-bit performance counter\n12. `rtl/ibex_pmp.sv` — PMP region checker (stub when PMPEnable=0)\n\n### Phase 3: Pipeline Sub-modules\n13. `rtl/ibex_fetch_fifo.sv` — 2-entry instruction FIFO\n14. `rtl/ibex_prefetch_buffer.sv` — Instruction prefetch manager\n15. `rtl/ibex_multdiv_fast.sv` — Fast multiplier/divider (DIT-aware)\n16. `rtl/ibex_multdiv_slow.sv` — Slow multiplier/divider (area-optimized)\n17. `rtl/ibex_ex_block.sv` — ALU + multdiv + jump result selector\n18. `rtl/ibex_load_store_unit.sv` — Load/store with byte-enables and alignment\n19. `rtl/ibex_wb_stage.sv` — Writeback stage (passthrough when WritebackStage=0)\n20. `rtl/ibex_dummy_instr.sv` — LFSR-based dummy instruction insertion\n21. `rtl/ibex_cs_registers.sv` — All CSRs incl. mstatus/mie/mtvec/mepc/mcause/mcycle/cpuctrlsts/PMP\n\n### Phase 4: Pipeline Stages\n22. `rtl/ibex_controller.sv` — Pipeline FSM (IDLE/FIRST_FETCH/DECODE/FLUSH states)\n23. `rtl/ibex_if_stage.sv` — Instruction fetch stage (prefetch + FIFO + compressed decoder)\n24. `rtl/ibex_id_stage.sv` — Decode + execute stage (decoder + controller + ex_block + LSU + regs)\n\n### Phase 5: Top-Level Integration\n25. `rtl/ibex_icache.sv` — ICache stub (ICache=0 default; must still compile)\n26. `rtl/ibex_lockstep.sv` — Lockstep stub (SecureIbex=0 default)\n27. `rtl/ibex_core.sv` — Integration of IF + ID stages + CSRs + PMP\n28. `rtl/ibex_top.sv` — Core + register file + lockstep\n29. `rtl/ibex_tracer.sv` — RVFI tracer\n30. `rtl/ibex_top_tracing.sv` — ibex_top + tracer\n31. `examples/simple_system/rtl/ibex_simple_system.sv` — SoC wrapper\n32. `examples/sw/simple_system/common/simple_system_common.c` — (already present; check if empty)\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n### Pipeline Architecture\n- 2-stage pipeline: IF stage and ID/EX stage\n- Instruction memory uses request/grant/rvalid handshaking\n- Data memory uses request/grant/rvalid handshaking\n- No WritebackStage (default=0): results written in ID/EX stage\n\n### CSR 0x7C0 (CPUCTRLSTS)\n```\nBit 0: icache_enable (for dit_test)\nBit 1: data_ind_timing / DIT (for dit_test)\nBit 2: dummy_instr_en (for dummy_instr_test)\nBit 5:3: dummy_instr_mask (frequency control)\n```\n\n### DIT Implementation\n- In ibex_multdiv_fast: when data_ind_timing=1, always run full 32 iterations (no early termination)\n- In ibex_controller: when data_ind_timing=1, taken branches add 1 extra bubble cycle\n\n### Dummy Instruction Implementation\n- ibex_dummy_instr uses prim_lfsr to generate pseudo-random insertion points\n- When dummy_instr_en=1, inserts NOP-like instructions at random intervals\n- This causes varying cycle counts between runs (different LFSR state each call)\n\n### Interrupt Handling (hello_test)\n- Vectored mode: mtvec.MODE=01, handler at BASE+4*cause\n- Timer interrupt: cause=7, handler at mtvec.BASE+0x1C\n- On interrupt: save PC to mepc, set mcause, jump to vector\n- On MRET: restore PC from mepc, restore privilege\n\n### Memory Map for simple_system\n```\n0x00100000 - 0x001FFFFF : RAM (1MB, boot offset 0x80 from base)\n0x00020000             : SIM_CTRL (char output + halt)\n0x00030000             : Timer (mtime/mtimecmp)\n```\n- Boot address: 0x00100000 (fetch starts at 0x00100080 = boot_addr + 0x80)\n\n## Critical Files\n\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_pkg.sv` — must define all enums referenced by other modules\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_cs_registers.sv` — complex, implements all CSRs\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_controller.sv` — complex FSM, drives pipeline flow\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_id_stage.sv` — integrates most pipeline sub-modules\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_core.sv` — top integration point\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_top.sv` — adds register file + lockstep\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_top_tracing.sv` — adds RVFI tracer\n- `/app/examples/simple_system/rtl/ibex_simple_system.sv` — SoC wrapper\n\n## Vendor Primitives Available (Ready to Use)\n- `prim_lfsr` — for dummy instruction LFSR\n- `prim_flop`, `prim_flop_en` — registered elements\n- `prim_count` — hardened counter\n- `prim_assert` — assertions (no-ops in simulation with VERILATOR defined)\n- `prim_mubi_pkg` — mubi types used in fetch_enable_i\n- `prim_ram_1p`, `prim_ram_2p` — RAM memories\n- All `prim_secded_*` — ECC (needed for ICacheECC)\n- `prim_clock_gating`, `prim_clock_mux2` — clock control\n\n## Verification Steps\n\n```bash\n# 1. instruction insertion via CPUCTRLSTS\n4. `pmp_smoke_test` — PMP region enforcement (passes even without PMP since PMPEnable=0 default)\n\n## Default Build Configuration (from .vc file)\n\n```\nRV32E=0, ICache=0, ICacheScramble=0, ICacheECC=0\nBranchTargetALU=0, WritebackStage=0, SecureIbex=0\nBranchPredictor=0, DbgTriggerEn=0\nPMPEnable=0, PMPGranularity=0, PMPNumRegions=4\nMHPMCounterNum=0, MHPMCounterWidth=40\nRVFI=1 (defined), RV32M=RV32MFast, RV32B=RV32BNone\nRV32ZC=RV32ZcaZcbZcmp, RegFile=RegFileFF\n```\n\n## Critical Findings\n\n- **pmp_smoke_test**: With PMPEnable=0 (default), PMP CSR writes are ignored, store succeeds normally, main returns 0 → PASSES\n- **dit_test with ICache**: requires DIT timing to be constant even without real ICache (works if instruction fetches are always 1-cycle in simulation)\n- **dummy_instr_test**: requires LFSR-based random dummy instruction insertion causing cycle-count variation\n- **hello_test**: requires timer interrupt at ID 7 (vectored mtvec: BASE+4*7), WFI, MMIO I/O\n\n## Implementation Order (bottom-up dependency)\n\n### Phase 1: Packages and Primitives\n1. `rtl/ibex_pkg.sv` — All enums, types, params. **Implement first.**\n2. `rtl/ibex_tracer_pkg.sv` — Tracer enum constants\n\n### Phase 2: Leaf Modules (no sub-module deps)\n3. `rtl/ibex_alu.sv` — ADD/SUB/AND/OR/XOR/shifts/compare, 32-bit\n4. `rtl/ibex_compressed_decoder.sv` — RVC→RVI expansion\n5. `rtl/ibex_decoder.sv` — 32-bit instruction decode → control signals\n6. `rtl/ibex_register_file_ff.sv` — 32×32 register file (FF-based)\n7. `rtl/ibex_register_file_fpga.sv` — FPGA variant (same interface, can be alias)\n8. `rtl/ibex_register_file_latch.sv` — Latch variant (same interface)\n9. `rtl/ibex_branch_predict.sv` — Stub (BranchPredictor=0 default)\n10. `rtl/ibex_csr.sv` — Single CSR primitive (WARL register with mask)\n11. `rtl/ibex_counter.sv` — 64-bit performance counter\n12. `rtl/ibex_pmp.sv` — PMP region checker (stub when PMPEnable=0)\n\n### Phase 3: Pipeline Sub-modules\n13. `rtl/ibex_fetch_fifo.sv` — 2-entry instruction FIFO\n14. `rtl/ibex_prefetch_buffer.sv` — Instruction prefetch manager\n15. `rtl/ibex_multdiv_fast.sv` — Fast multiplier/divider (DIT-aware)\n16. `rtl/ibex_multdiv_slow.sv` — Slow multiplier/divider (area-optimized)\n17. `rtl/ibex_ex_block.sv` — ALU + multdiv + jump result selector\n18. `rtl/ibex_load_store_unit.sv` — Load/store with byte-enables and alignment\n19. `rtl/ibex_wb_stage.sv` — Writeback stage (passthrough when WritebackStage=0)\n20. `rtl/ibex_dummy_instr.sv` — LFSR-based dummy instruction insertion\n21. `rtl/ibex_cs_registers.sv` — All CSRs incl. mstatus/mie/mtvec/mepc/mcause/mcycle/cpuctrlsts/PMP\n\n### Phase 4: Pipeline Stages\n22. `rtl/ibex_controller.sv` — Pipeline FSM (IDLE/FIRST_FETCH/DECODE/FLUSH states)\n23. `rtl/ibex_if_stage.sv` — Instruction fetch stage (prefetch + FIFO + compressed decoder)\n24. `rtl/ibex_id_stage.sv` — Decode + execute stage (decoder + controller + ex_block + LSU + regs)\n\n### Phase 5: Top-Level Integration\n25. `rtl/ibex_icache.sv` — ICache stub (ICache=0 default; must still compile)\n26. `rtl/ibex_lockstep.sv` — Lockstep stub (SecureIbex=0 default)\n27. `rtl/ibex_core.sv` — Integration of IF + ID stages + CSRs + PMP\n28. `rtl/ibex_top.sv` — Core + register file + lockstep\n29. `rtl/ibex_tracer.sv` — RVFI tracer\n30. `rtl/ibex_top_tracing.sv` — ibex_top + tracer\n31. `examples/simple_system/rtl/ibex_simple_system.sv` — SoC wrapper\n32. `examples/sw/simple_system/common/simple_system_common.c` — (already present; check if empty)\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n### Pipeline Architecture\n- 2-stage pipeline: IF stage and ID/EX stage\n- Instruction memory uses request/grant/rvalid handshaking\n- Data memory uses request/grant/rvalid handshaking\n- No WritebackStage (default=0): results written in ID/EX stage\n\n### CSR 0x7C0 (CPUCTRLSTS)\n```\nBit 0: icache_enable (for dit_test)\nBit 1: data_ind_timing / DIT (for dit_test)\nBit 2: dummy_instr_en (for dummy_instr_test)\nBit 5:3: dummy_instr_mask (frequency control)\n```\n\n### DIT Implementation\n- In ibex_multdiv_fast: when data_ind_timing=1, always run full 32 iterations (no early termination)\n- In ibex_controller: when data_ind_timing=1, taken branches add 1 extra bubble cycle\n\n### Dummy Instruction Implementation\n- ibex_dummy_instr uses prim_lfsr to generate pseudo-random insertion points\n- When dummy_instr_en=1, inserts NOP-like instructions at random intervals\n- This causes varying cycle counts between runs (different LFSR state each call)\n\n### Interrupt Handling (hello_test)\n- Vectored mode: mtvec.MODE=01, handler at BASE+4*cause\n- Timer interrupt: cause=7, handler at mtvec.BASE+0x1C\n- On interrupt: save PC to mepc, set mcause, jump to vector\n- On MRET: restore PC from mepc, restore privilege\n\n### Memory Map for simple_system\n```\n0x00100000 - 0x001FFFFF : RAM (1MB, boot offset 0x80 from base)\n0x00020000             : SIM_CTRL (char output + halt)\n0x00030000             : Timer (mtime/mtimecmp)\n```\n- Boot address: 0x00100000 (fetch starts at 0x00100080 = boot_addr + 0x80)\n\n## Critical Files\n\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_pkg.sv` — must define all enums referenced by other modules\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_cs_registers.sv` — complex, implements all CSRs\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_controller.sv` — complex FSM, drives pipeline flow\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_id_stage.sv` — integrates most pipeline sub-modules\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_core.sv` — top integration point\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_top.sv` — adds register file + lockstep\n- `/app/rtl/ibex_top_tracing.sv` — adds RVFI tracer\n- `/app/examples/simple_system/rtl/ibex_simple_system.sv` — SoC wrapper\n\n## Vendor Primitives Available (Ready to Use)\n- `prim_lfsr` — for dummy instruction LFSR\n- `prim_flop`, `prim_flop_en` — registered elements\n- `prim_count` — hardened counter\n- `prim_assert` — assertions (no-ops in simulation with VERILATOR defined)\n- `prim_mubi_pkg` — mubi types used in fetch_enable_i\n- `prim_ram_1p`, `prim_ram_2p` — RAM memories\n- All `prim_secded_*` — ECC (needed for ICacheECC)\n- `prim_clock_gating`, `prim_clock_mux2` — clock control\n\n## Verification Steps\n\n```bash\n# 1. Compile all SW tests\nfor t in hello_test dit_test dummy_instr_test pmp_smoke_test; do\n    cd /app/examples/sw/simple_system/$t && make && cd /app\ndone\n\n# 2. Build simulator\nfusesoc --cores-root=. run --target=sim --build lowrisc:ibex:ibex_simple_system\n\n# 3. Run all tests\nSIM=build/lowrisc_ibex_ibex_simple_system_0/sim-verilator/Vibex_simple_system\nfor t in hello_test dit_test dummy_instr_test pmp_smoke_test; do\n    echo \"=== $t ===\" && \"$SIM\" --raminit=\"examples/sw/simple_system/$t/${t}.vmem\" && echo PASSED || echo FAILED\ndone\n```\n\n## Scope Notes\n- ICache: implement as stub (compile-clean, ICache=0 default, no real caching behavior needed for tests)\n- Lockstep: implement as stub (SecureIbex=0 default)\n- RVFI: implement tracing signals (RVFI=1 is always defined in ibex_top_tracing)\n- Register file FPGA/latch variants: can be identical to FF variant for simulation purposes\n- BranchTargetALU: stub (default=0, branch target computed in ALU)\n- WritebackStage: stub/passthrough (default=0)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

restore PC from mepc, restore privilege\n\n### Memory Map for simple_system\n```\n0x00100000 - 0x001FFFFF : RAM (1MB, boot offset 0x80 from base)\n0x00020000             : SIM_CTRL (char output +
